- **choose-loop-wakeup-interval**: Select a `delaySeconds` value when scheduling a loop wakeup via the `ScheduleWakeup` tool or the `/loop` slash command. Covers the three-tier cache-aware decision (cache-warm under 5 minutes, cache-miss 5 minutes to 1 hour, idle default 20 to 30 minutes), the 300-second anti-pattern, the [60, 3600] runtime clamp, the minute-boundary rounding quirk, and writing a telemetry-worthy `reason` field. Use when designing an autonomous loop, when a heartbeat cadence is being planned, when polling cadence is being tuned, or when post-hoc review of loop costs reveals interval mis-sizing. ([pjt222/agent-almanac](https://github.com/pjt222/agent-almanac/tree/HEAD/i18n/caveman/skills/choose-loop-wakeup-interval) · ★34 · 10 copies)
- **choose-loop-wakeup-interval**: 通过 `ScheduleWakeup` 工具或 `/loop` 斜杠命令调度循环唤醒时，选择 `delaySeconds` 值。涵盖三档缓存感知决策（缓存温热小于 5 分钟、 缓存未命中 5 分钟到 1 小时、空闲默认 20 到 30 分钟）、300 秒反模式、 [60, 3600] 运行时夹紧、分钟边界舍入特性，以及编写有遥测价值的 `reason` 字段。在设计自主循环、规划心跳节奏、调优轮询节奏，或事后审查循环成本 发现间隔尺寸不当时使用。 ([pjt222/agent-almanac](https://github.com/pjt222/agent-almanac/tree/HEAD/i18n/zh-CN/skills/choose-loop-wakeup-interval) · ★34)
